# Per-video metric report

32 videos · each corruption shown with its clean baseline (severity 0)

_Recall_ = trustworthy headline (partial GT). _Precision/MOTA_ = soft (partial GT). _IDF1/HOTA/AssA_ = identity quality (sustained swaps mostly forgiven). _IDsw_ = swap events (high on yolo = detection dropouts). `gtdet` = perfect boxes (isolates tracking/ReID); `yolo` = real detector. See summary.md for full glossary.
